# Fanout report -- module `dcache`

_Generated by `tests/Stages/Harish_StageTesting/fanout/scripts/fanout_optimize.py`._

> **Note on `inv_N$`**: as of this report, `lib/STDCells/UniqueLib.v` still wraps `inv1$` (drive ~1) inside `inv_N$`, **not** `bufferHInv16$`. Many recommendations assume the macro will be upgraded. If the macro has already been changed in another branch, ignore this note. Otherwise, either upgrade the macro globally (clears every inverter-driven violation at once) or instantiate `bufferHInv*$` explicitly per violation.

- Total VIOLATIONs in check.log: **363**
- In-scope (driver inside `dcache`): **267**

## In-scope violations (267)

### 1. `q[0]` (fanout=15) — driven by `reg64e$`

- **Driver path**: (Everywhere_TOP) → dcache_unit(DCache_TOP) → dcache_arbitration(DCache_Arbitration) → g_arb_block[3].u_reg_oe(MPS_reg_rst_we$_WIDTH1) → gen_reg64[0].u_reg(reg64e$)
- **Required tier**: `bufferH16$ / bufferHInv16$`

| # | Option | Added delay | Action |
|---|---|---|---|
| **✅ recommended** | Register duplication × 4 | 0.00 ns | In `<file for DCache_Arbitration>` clone `g_arb_block[3].u_reg_oe` into 4 copies (`g_arb_block[3].u_reg_oe_a` … `g_arb_block[3].u_reg_oe_d`); each copy drives ~4 loads. **Output side**: per-copy fanout = 4 → no per-copy buffer. **Input side (D)**: fanout = 4 on the next-state wire → no input buffer. clk/rst are clock-tree distributed so they don't count. Total added delay on a load path = 0.00 + 0.00 = **0.00 ns**. This assumes D was fanout-1 before duplication; if the next-state expression already feeds other loads, the input-side cost may be smaller (existing buffer reused) or larger (extra cascade). |
| alt 1 | Buffer with bufferHInv16$ (polarity flip needed) | 0.15 ns | Use `bufferHInv16$` (single inverter, **0.15 ns**) only if every downstream consumer of `g_arb_block[3].u_reg_oe.q` can absorb the inversion (NAND↔AND swap, mux input swap, etc.). Otherwise pair-cost = 0.30 ns and `bufferH16$` is cheaper. |
| alt 2 | Buffer with bufferH16$ | 0.24 ns | Insert `bufferH16$` between `g_arb_block[3].u_reg_oe.q` and its loads in `<file for DCache_Arbitration>`. The buffer's input is a single load on `g_arb_block[3].u_reg_oe.q` (fanout-1, no upstream cascade). Adds **0.24 ns** on every path through this signal. |
